### greet-zh

```

### direct-ja

```
--- step 1: user says: 一言で：TCP と UDP の一番の違いは何ですか？
  [partner message] TCPは信頼性、UDPは速度を優先する違いです。TCPは届いたか確認して再送し順序も保証しますが、UDPは確認なしに送るので遅延が少なく軽い代わりに届かないこともあります。
```

### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Usa rebase para poner al día tu rama local, con historial lineal, antes de integrarla, pero solo si nadie más ha usado esos commits. Usa merge cuando la rama ya es compartida o quieres conservar el registro real de cuándo se unió el trabajo.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 是可靠的、面向连接的传输，保证数据按序、不丢不重地送达；UDP 则是无连接、尽力而为的传输，速度快、开销小，但不保证送达和顺序。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase when you want a clean, linear history for a branch only you are working on, before it's shared. Merge when the branch is shared or public, or when you want to keep the record of when work was combined.
```

bun test -t "登录失败"

### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log is a file where every change to the database is written down before it's applied to the main data, so a crash mid-write can be replayed or rolled back and nothing committed is lost. It trades a little extra writing up front for a database that recovers reliably and can batch its slower writes to disk.
```

  var runsEl = document.getElementById('runs');
  var latestW = document.getElementById('latest-writes');
  var latestSub = document.getElementById('latest-sub');

    var data = ducter.get('runs') || [];
    runsEl.innerHTML = '';

      runsEl.innerHTML = '<div class="k-empty">No nightly runs yet.</div>';
      latestW.textContent = '—';
      latestSub.textContent = 'Writes per second';
      return;
    }
    var last = data[data.length - 1];
    latestW.textContent = last.writes + '/s';
    latestSub.textContent = 'p99 ' + last.p99 + ' · compaction stalls: ' + last.stalls;
    data.slice().reverse().forEach(function(r){
      var row = document.createElement('div');
      row.className = 'k-row';
      row.innerHTML = '<div><div class="k-row-main">' + r.night + '</div><div class="k-row-sub">p99 ' + r.p99 + ' · stalls ' + r.stalls + '</div></div><div class="k-row-value">' + r.writes + '/s</div>';
      runsEl.appendChild(row);
    });
  }
  ducter.onChange(render);
  render();
})();
</script>

  function todayStr() {
    var d = new Date();
    return d.getFullYear() + '-' + p2(d.getMonth() + 1) + '-' + p2(d.getDate());
  }

  function esc(s) {

  document.getElementById('save').addEventListener('click', function () {
    var day = document.getElementById('d').value.trim() || todayStr();
    var what = document.getElementById('w').value.trim();
    var amount = parseFloat(document.getElementById('a').value);
    if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(day)) return;
    if (!what || isNaN(amount) || amount <= 0) return;
    items.push({
      id: Date.now().toString(36) + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 6),
      day: day,
      what: what,
      amount: Math.round(amount * 100) / 100
    });
    document.getElementById('w').value = '';
    document.getElementById('a').value = '';
    persist(); render();
  });

  document.getElementById('d').value = todayStr();
  try { ducter.onChange(function () { load(); }); } catch (e) {}
  load();
})();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {}

  <div class="k-sum-label">合计</div>

  <div class="k-sum-sub" id="count">还没记</div>

<div class="k-card"><div class="k-list" id="list"></div></div>

<script>
function draw(){
  const xs = ducter.get("expenses") || [];
  const sum = xs.reduce((s, x) => s + (Number(x.yuan) || 0), 0);
  document.getElementById("total").textContent = "¥" + sum.toLocaleString("zh-CN");
  document.getElementById("count").textContent = xs.length ? xs.length + " 笔" : "还没记";
  const list = document.getElementById("list");
  list.innerHTML = "";
  for (const x of xs) {
    const row = document.createElement("div");
    row.className = "k-row";
    const main = document.createElement("div");
    main.className = "k-row-main";
    main.textContent = x.what;
    const sub = document.createElement("div");
    sub.className = "k-row-sub";
    sub.textContent = x.day;
    main.appendChild(sub);
    const val = document.createElement("div");
    val.className = "k-row-value";
    val.textContent = "¥" + x.yuan;
    row.appendChild(main);
    row.appendChild(val);
    list.appendChild(row);
  }
}
ducter.onChange(draw);
draw();
const _xs = ducter.get("expenses") || [];
ducter.summary(_xs.length + " 笔,合计 ¥" + _xs.reduce((s, x) => s + (Number(x.yuan) || 0), 0));
</script>

</main>
<script>
const KEY = 'feeds';
let feeds = [];
const $ = id => document.getElementById(id);
function today() {
  const d = new Date();
  return d.getFullYear() + '-' + String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0') + '-' + String(d.getDate()).padStart(2, '0');
}
function nowHM() {
  const d = new Date();
  return String(d.getHours()).padStart(2, '0') + ':' + String(d.getMinutes()).padStart(2, '0');
}
function load() {
  const v = ducter.get(KEY);
  feeds = Array.isArray(v) ? v : [];
}
function save() { ducter.set(KEY, feeds); }
function render() {
  const t = today();
  const list = feeds.filter(f => f.date === t).sort((a, b) => a.time.localeCompare(b.time));
  const sum = list.reduce((s, f) => s + f.ml, 0);
  $('total').innerHTML = list.length ? sum + ' ml' : '<span class="k-none">还没记</span>';
  $('count').textContent = list.length ? list.length + ' 次' : '今天还没有喝奶记录';
  $('list').innerHTML = list.length
    ? list.map(f => '<div class="k-row" data-id="' + f.id + '"><div class="k-row-main">' + f.time + '</div><div class="k-row-value">' + f.ml + ' ml</div></div>').join('')
    : '<div class="k-empty">今天的第一顿记在这里</div>';
  ducter.summary(list.length ? '今天 ' + list.length + ' 次,合计 ' + sum + 'ml' : '今天还没记');
}
$('list').addEventListener('click', e => {
  const row = e.target.closest('.k-row');
  if (!row) return;
  feeds = feeds.filter(f => String(f.id) !== row.dataset.id);
  save(); render();
});
$('add').addEventListener('click', () => {
  const ml = parseFloat($('ml').value);
  if (!(ml > 0)) return;
  let time = $('t').value.trim() || nowHM();
  if (!/^\d{1,2}:\d{2}$/.test(time)) return;
  const [h, m] = time.split(':');
  time = String(h).padStart(2, '0') + ':' + m;
  feeds.push({ id: Date.now(), date: today(), time, ml });
  $('t').value = ''; $('ml').value = '';
  save(); render();
});
ducter.onChange(() => { load(); render(); });
load(); render();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"feeds":[{"id":1,"date":"2026-11-24","time":"06:10","ml":120},{"id":2,"date":"2026-11-24","time":"09:05","ml":90}]}
  [also used tools] app
```

### app-prs-known-en

```
(earlier) user: which of my PRs are still waiting on someone?
(earlier) partner: Four: wey-gu/ducter#412 (compaction cut) waits on Weizhen's review since Monday; nowledge-co/mem#10287 (retry on 502) on CI, failing; nowledge-co/hawdb#1592 (flaky compaction test) on Alice since Tuesday; wey-gu/ducter#418 (apps bar) is a draft.
--- step 1: user says: make that a little page I can keep and tick off as they land
  [partner message] Done: a page with the four PRs, and a "Mark landed" tap on each that moves the count as they land. It's on your shelf under the PR icon.
  [an app card under the message: made "PRs waiting on others", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 2214 characters): <main class="k-app">
  <div class="k-sum">
    <div class="k-sum-label">Still waiting</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="count">4</div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">of 4 PRs · tap one when it lands</div>
  </div>
  <h2 class="k-h">PRs waiting on someone</h2>
  <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
</main>
<script>
(function () {
  var PRS = ducter.get("prs") || [];
  var done = {};

  function render() {
    var list = document.getElementById('list');
    list.innerHTML = '';
    var landed = 0;
    PRS.forEach(function (p) {
      var on = !!done[p.id];
      if (on) landed++;
      var row = document.createElement('div');
      row.className = 'k-row';
      var btn = document.createElement('button');
      btn.className = 'k-chip';
      btn.setAttribute('aria-pressed', on ? 'true' : 'false');
      btn.textContent = on ? '✓ Landed' : 'Mark landed';
      btn.onclick = function () {
        done[p.id] = !done[p.id];
        save();
        render();
      };
      var main = document.createElement('div');
      main.className = 'k-row-main';
      main.innerHTML = '<strong></strong><div class="k-row-sub"></div>';
      main.querySelector('strong').textContent = p.repo + ' · ' + p.title;
      main.querySelector('.k-row-sub').textContent = 'Waiting on: ' + p.wait;
      row.appendChild(main);
      row.appendChild(btn);
      list.appendChild(row);
    });
    var waiting = PRS.length - landed;
    document.getElementById('count').textContent = String(waiting);
    document.getElementById('sub').textContent = 'of ' + PRS.length + ' PRs · ' + landed + ' landed';
  }

  function save() {
    try { ducter.set('done', done); } catch (e) {}
    try {
      var waiting = PRS.filter(function (p) { return !done[p.id]; }).length;
      var landed = PRS.length - waiting;
      ducter.summary(waiting + ' waiting, ' + landed + ' landed');
    } catch (e) {}
  }

  try {
    Promise.resolve(ducter.get('done')).then(function (v) {
      done = v || {};
      render();
    });
    ducter.onChange(function () {
      Promise.resolve(ducter.get('done')).then(function (v) {
        done = v || {};
        render();
      });
    });
  } catch (e) {
    render();
  }
})();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"prs":[{"id":"ducter412","repo":"wey-gu/ducter#412","title":"Compaction cut","wait":"Weizhen’s review, since Monday"},{"id":"mem10287","repo":"nowledge-co/mem#10287","title":"Retry on 502","wait":"CI, failing"},{"id":"hawdb1592","repo":"nowledge-co/hawdb#1592","title":"Flaky compaction test","wait":"Alice, since Tuesday"},{"id":"ducter418","repo":"wey-gu/ducter#418","title":"Apps bar","wait":"Still a draft"}]}
  [also used tools] app
```

  [partner ack line] 好，我让那个 TTL 任务再补一条正好到期那一刻的测试，补完告诉你。
  [sent to task ttl] 用户原话："这个再补一条测试，覆盖正好到期那一刻"

要做的：在现有的 TTL 测试基础上，再补一条测试，覆盖缓存项恰好到期的那一刻。沿用已经用上的假时钟，把时间推进到正好等于 TTL 的时刻，断言此时该值已经过期（或按现有约定的边界语义，断言它仍然有效），并同时覆盖刚好差一点点的前一刻。

完成标准：新测试通过；整套测试连续多次运行都稳定通过；在报告里贴出测试输出和新增测试的断言内容。

不做的事：不合并、不推送、不开 PR。分支上照常提交即可。

地点：~/dev/foo，分支 ducter/foo-ttl。
